Before selected-folder support, retain a structured lease for the real graph and scope the foreground reconciliation latch to each activation; do not reuse this default-only shortcut for a switch.

The [activation-scoped OCR receipt](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#library-scoped-ocr-delivery) passed 6/6 on `35e5519`, with the exact receipt and package bytes rechecked. The default pointer was not switched, unrelated guest state was not reset, and both owned processes exited and released. The next path remains completion/acknowledgment of a fresh backup without a provider replay, followed by the selected-graph lifetime and qualified writer.

While the exact OCR pair is in its authorized runtime batch, the independent host-only backup increment starts with a bounded private intent/receipt format, not a new provider writer. A fresh explicit confirmation supplies one process-owned operation ID and issuing activation; the actual Files result supplies the exact tree and modes. Before creating a backup child, the private record binds those values, the observed parent identity and the exact closed catalog generation. A lost result can distinguish an intent without completion from an exact completed receipt and a separately acknowledged receipt. The record alone does not prove present provider access or physical identity, and recovery grants no write/replay authority. Keep prior test records, original data and Settings unchanged. Recheck after the first record tests or the native OCR receipt, whichever comes first.

Independent journal review found two additional Important faults: an unlinked terminal stage could be treated as a clean predecessor, and the candidate catalog could change in the private intent writer's later directory barrier. Both reproduced as JVM assertion failures, in addition to the earlier leftover-before-intent and changed-during-stage-force controls. Bind completion to a live, one-shot token issued only after the exact closed stage and durable intent are verified. Reopen may re-force an exact final record with a surviving link to the same file; it cannot ignore an unexplained stage, stage another completion or open the provider. The Android caller must still hold the issuing activation and actual proof of provider publication; a private receipt reconstructed after interruption is not that proof.

The private journal increment passed 1,007 data and 271 app JVM cases, both affected use paths compiled, and 237 host storage checks passed. The fourteen focused cases include an independently calculated 571-byte intent vector, the maximum permitted exact URI, no read-only authorization, ordered records, both uncertain journal faults and retained same-inode recovery. The corrected writer forces its already verified open catalog, checks it immediately before linking the record, and rechecks before releasing the live token. Only the private filesystem and injected barriers were exercised. Next: bind the stable operation to the actual Files result and default-store activation, consume the live token only after provider readback, and distinguish recovering/acknowledging a historical receipt from replaying a write; keep Settings and retention promotion gated on that witness.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01` backup-receipt consumer from `f82a594`: one default-store export operation, the exact actual Files result, the existing bounded provider publisher and private intent/completion/acknowledgment store. Scope: the export adapter and store facade, synthetic app-test witnesses, and contract/evidence. The missing methods reproduced at Android test compilation. The decisive witness is to publish only one backup child, preserve the original and active pointer, inspect and acknowledge the exact receipt after loss of write access, and refuse the repeated operation ID without opening a second provider writer. Acknowledgment is private bookkeeping, not a renewed claim of access. One offline build slot and one retained app-guest batch; checkpoint at review or build outcome, within 30 active minutes. The Settings confirmation owner, actual process-death/recovery and capacity refusal remain open; switchable-graph, selected-root publication and retirement are not prerequisites for this default-only witness and are not qualified by it.

Independent consumer review found no Critical defect and two Important gaps. Canceling immediately before linking `export.ack` could strand an unexplained private stage and hide an otherwise completed receipt; the isolated journal assertion reproduced that refusal. The bounded, already admitted acknowledgment now observes cancellation before staging and again after the exclusive-link write and directory barrier, not between a forced stage and its link. The native fixture now cancels result delivery at the real coroutine dispatcher boundary after the private completion exists, and rejects resolver entry during replay, stale admission and historical acknowledgment. It separately asserts both absence of an attempt and unchanged grants and folder inventory on early refusals. This is a canceled delivery, not an actual process death. Ruling: preserve ambiguous records and keep the bounded four-attempt cap for this internal adapter. Settings still needs owned retention and an activation-scoped, genuine one-shot confirmation; the raw request factory is not proof of human consent or a graph lease.

The corrected strict-offline candidate passed 1,900 JVM and 237 host storage checks, with the acknowledgment regression and both development packages assembled and inspected. The exact-pair runner is the only next device entry. The following selected-graph dependency is to connect the shared renderer to the existing scoped seekable original: split retained binding from file access, retain the producer's exact row check, and close provisional decoded output if the reader rejects delivery after decode. Reuse the ordinary default reader and existing selected-tree loan, not a second decoder or an unscoped scratch `File`. An authenticated read-only graph must still refuse saved writes until a qualified selected-root publisher exists.

The [lost-result API 28 receipt](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#lost-backup-result-and-private-acknowledgment) passed 10/10 on `1109e95`; exact installed package hashes and outcomes were rechecked and the app-test guest exited and released. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01` scoped decoder from `95f8d0b`: retain the existing producer lookup and exact source/normalized-pixel comparison, move file work into a single structured loan and transfer only the separately owned decoded raster after the lender acknowledges it. Both loan tests and their existing controls passed in the strict-offline 285 image, 1,008 data and 271 app cases; both dependent Android test sets compiled, no new emulator operation. The next use should pair the selected Room catalog and loan under its recorded activation, verify pointer drift after native decode, and refuse to reuse a port once its catalog scope is retired. Keep actual Settings selection and provider writes unenabled.

Selected-reader increment from `256a231`: admit the original only from the same verified catalog and provider view as the working Room scope; the ordinary decoder owns provisional pixels and the working-catalog gate covers the file loan through cleanup. The observed 12.15 GiB does not yet cover both; build and host verification may continue without touching a guest.

Independent selected-reader review found four Important issues: an owned raster could escape as a callback result and leak, an already retiring or changed scope could lend a file after spooling, definite provider loss could allow the same scope to revive, and the native witness did not bind the reopened page/revision to the recorded activation and exact source rows. Keep original-bearing public callback results `Unit`, check the scope against the selection at lending and after use, and latch only definite observed grant/tip loss from the loan. The revised Files fixture seeds an isolated private pointer for the real backup child, checks its current page/revision and source rows, rejects a differing creation-time row before opening the file, and changes its private selection after authentic native decode. The older pointer-seeded catalog-only case is not an editable graph. Temporary capacity recovered by compressing 22 link-free, repeated-byte build fixtures in place, with every pre/post hash equal (11.81 → 13.83 GiB at measurement); no APK or retained user data was changed. The initial corrected compilation hit the known Kotlin temporary-backup symptom (INC-008); the unchanged retry must verify before package admission.

The unchanged retry assembled, but the separate provider-contention control then reproduced permanent false invalidation when the shared provider lane was temporarily busy. The lane now reports that specific refusal without latching it as a lost folder; cancellation and ordinary codec failures are likewise distinct from definite loss. The next native candidate must include this correction. Review the real recorded-selection and post-decode refusal in the same app-guest batch before advancing the graph consumer. Selected publication, capacity refusal for a backup too large for the destination, and mandatory complete-portability tests remain open.

Independent default-lease review found two Important issues: the generic application borrower could return a graph or raw port, and save enrollment collapsed a refused activation lease into Room's legitimate no-target result, falsely completing the notification. Independent foreground-latch review found two Important issues: the existing native save witness reflected the old enum and would fail before testing save enrollment, and the new STARTED hint could open or retry writable Room independently of ordinary library startup. Closed-title admission from `278c540`: the portable reader and source snapshot could admit outer whitespace that the ordinary catalog rejects. Independent title review found one Important replay fault: SQLite's UTF-16 bind strips one leading U+FEFF, so repeated staging could refuse an already accepted title rather than preserve it. An in-memory native SQLite probe reproduced the lost code unit and verified that a UTF-8 BLOB cast back to TEXT preserves it. The actual Room-schema host query initially failed for the missing byte-preserving insert and now exercises the exact statement. Retain targeted native cases for padded source capture, padded closed projection and two fresh reopens preserving leading U+FEFF, decomposed text and embedded NUL. Do not change historical exact-candidate runner manifests to pretend these cases already ran. Recheck at the strict-offline result, then prioritize preserving executable Undo through a real restored catalog rather than adding an incomplete Settings route.

After review corrections, all 1,014 data and 278 app JVM cases and 238 host storage checks passed; the three affected data Android witnesses compile and remain unrun. The next host-verifiable boundary is paired Undo admission: validate both before/after actions and exact retained owners, then rebuild pins in a reopened catalog and invoke the ordinary Undo. The runtime block stays separate from the title correction.

Paired Undo boundary from `955e789`: validate both canonical halves as one edit against that album's retained pages and recipe rows. Reuse the existing ordinary membership checks instead of authoring a parallel definition. This slice is bounded to the codec, paired validator, ordinary membership validator reuse, focused host tests and contract. First RED was the absent paired validator. The next consumer must bound the retained rows and BLOBs before reading, preserve the existing expiry and ordering (including applied/expired rank occupancy), construct pins from the admitted owners and verify the resulting rows and counters after reopen. A same-title merge must not promote a stale Undo or silently discard an actionable membership command that would be made unexecutable by append. Preserve affected input separately until that behavior is supported. No current portable file includes executable Undo merely because this validator exists.

Independent paired-Undo review found no Critical issue and reproduced two Important admissions the ordinary writers never emit: a no-op reorder and a batch history containing a byte-identical page transition. The corrections are portable-only; ordinary single-page save can append identical bytes and remains admissible. The witnesses use genuine ordinary reorder, multi-page batch and single-save records, and verify the pin for a removed page whose source is not shared by survivors.

The corrected strict-offline run recorded 1,923 JVM cases without failures or skips (1,021 data, including seven focused Undo cases), with data Android test compilation. The fresh Android fixture includes a rank-evicted AVAILABLE row behind APPLIED/EXPIRED occupants and a request at the exact expiry boundary. This witness is not counted as run.

Reviewed private stage kernel: 1,030 data JVM cases passed, including all 16 journal cases, and app Android test compilation succeeded. Settings owner and route-exit tests passed in 292 app JVM cases; app Android tests compiled, and the seven runner checks passed. Keep the still-unqualified Settings changes in the local worktree. Settings review correction, pending final evidence: use a live exact Files capability without taking or releasing persistent modes. The missing ancestry admission reproduced at data test compilation; the four focused JVM cases then passed with no failures or skips. The independent transfer review found one Important issue: phase-level checks allowed another original to open or a staged object to become visible after proof was lost. The correction checks before and after both stream opens, closes any refused stream, and requires the destination adapter to recheck at stage creation and immediately before visibility. The same strict-offline run passed all 31 focused merge, transfer, root policy and copy/replay tests. A refused stage may remain as evidence; no predecessor or source is removed. This does not cover the selected provider's own write/visibility calls, catalog append or retirement.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, granted descriptor ancestry from `9355224`: first prove (or get a definite refusal from) the existing descriptor walk on actual organized Files children, under one selected synthetic root as the independently supplied anchor. Independent ancestry review found two Important evidence gaps. Run syncable-descriptor and ancestry stages with the same selected URI and permission lifecycle, and require effective platform write denial for both exact URI spellings before starting either probe after Activity recreation. Keep the control read-only and recheck grant state after use. The debug-only bridge keeps the production kernel internal. Check a second descriptor's complete mount/device/inode identity before treating it as an alias and recheck the enumerated locators and held identities after the walks. This narrows the witness to two children (`catalogs` and `objects`) under one anchor. It does not establish immediate parent edges, stability under concurrent mutation or deletion authority.

The [first ancestry run](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#granted-descriptor-ancestry) reproduced effective write permission after recreation and explicitly refused to enter either descriptor stage. Finish the chooser-owning Activity instead and require the same before/after permission assertions on the persisted read grant. Verify only this corrected test on the unchanged app and exact released predecessor; leave the initial target intact. Recheck at the native result or within 30 active minutes; if the grant still cannot be isolated, do not weaken the witness or change product permissions to force a pass.

The Activity finish was insufficient on `c458b00`; neither descriptor stage ran. Reuse the already qualified cold-process pattern: acquire exactly one persisted read grant on a fresh fixture, stop only the verified test app, verify process absence, and run both probes headlessly after confirming both URI spellings deny write. Release only the newly retained exact read mode in the cold test. Recheck both refused fixtures and all current fixture bytes; no VM restart between the two instrumentations, no provider writes and no change to application permissions. If a cold process also sees effective write, report that boundary and advance the independent capacity/interruption work rather than repeating the same trial.

`dd764d5` did not reach the cold process: its pre-picker empty-inventory assertion saw an unrelated persisted grant. Preserve that grant; compare the exact fixture read delta and a deterministic digest of all unrelated URI/mode bindings across the handoff and cleanup. Authenticate the pre-picker refusal and retain its fixture alongside the two earlier destinations. Checkpoint at the first actual cold-process permission or ancestry outcome; no unchanged retry, storage reset or release claim.

`321e1ea` passed the Files read handoff; the host withheld cold launch after a package-matching PID appeared after the scoped stop. Instead of opening Files for a fourth grant trial, authenticate the exact passed handoff and finish only its unrun headless test with the same already installed APK pair and retained fixture. Verify both processes are absent before launch, preserving both the unrelated URI grants and all previous files. Checkpoint at the native result; if denied at a later boundary, record it without relaxing the test or enabling selected-folder writes.

The [granted descriptor ancestry witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#granted-descriptor-ancestry) passed on `15d39b8`: the cold process recovered the one synthetic Files read grant, proved effective write denial and observed disjoint, same-mount ancestry for two managed children of its held root. Reopened descriptors matched, aliases were refused, and the temporary persisted read grant was released without touching the prior inventory. Do not repeat the completed one-root test. The next topology gate is independent exact provider locators for the two roots a real operation would use, and revalidation under a qualified writer owner. In parallel sequencing, retain true interrupted-publication and definite capacity-refusal witnesses for the default backup; no selected write, live cutover, or retirement qualifies from this result.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, real provider capacity admission from `5beda95`: exercise the production provider's second capacity check on the exact synthetic Files result, with a synthetic request greater than the entire descriptor's reported mounted volume. Do not fill a disk, write a private intent, or start publication; the intent callback is a tripwire and the host verifies bytes and inventories before/after. Scope: debug-only observer, exact picker witness, runner and host checks; no app release behavior, schema or grant policy changes. The observer's absence reproduced at Android compilation. Check this negative provider witness within one bounded run after offline checks; a pass does not prove the complete Settings refusal/closed-stage cleanup or replay behavior, which require a separate test.

Independent capacity review found no Critical issues and two Important admission gaps: prove effective write denial for both exact spellings before Files opens, and bind the owned allocator record to the anchored predecessor before any APK replacement or fixture staging. Apply both and check the full synthetic inventory, including names and bytes, symmetrically before and after. The debug-only boundary supplies a real measured capacity comparison, not an actual low-disk condition or a Settings-level recovery test.

The [native provider capacity refusal](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#native-provider-capacity-refusal) passed on `0649338`. Real Files access and Android's measured capacity led to a definite pre-intent refusal for an unattainable synthetic requirement; neither fixture changed. Do not fill the retained guest to repeat this coverage. The next backup boundary is a real process interruption after exact payload verification but before the completion marker, with retained private intent/spends and no history success or repeat publication. Keep the full Settings low-capacity/cleanup and independently granted selected-root gates open.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, pre-marker interruption from `831d426`: first pin a single synchronous observation after exact catalog readback and the destination payload barriers, only for a new marker immediately before its creation. The JVM control must prove that an interruption at that point leaves the previous catalog and the newly verified payload intact with no marker, and that history-only verification never calls it. Then bind the observation per invocation through the ordinary backup writer to a debug-only process-death witness; no global fault injection, release UI, auto-retry or cleanup of prior results. Use one strict-offline build lane; recheck at RED/GREEN and independent persistence review, before requesting or using any new one-shot publication scope.

Independent publication review found no Critical or Important boundary issue; the no-observer path gained an extra cancellation observation. The focused host run reproduced that difference (one of eleven tests failed) and confirmed that cancellation requested by the observer already withheld the marker. Keep the second check only after a real observer returns, preserving the ordinary path and historical verification. The tests are local filesystem simulations, not provider durability or actual process death.

`cd54e50` passed the full 1,054-case data JVM suite. The Android continuation uses the ordinary store and writer through a debug-only, per-invocation stop observer. Its distinct checkpoint carries the exact operation, prospective marker IDs and source/recipe evidence before self-termination. The isolated runner spends only one new synthetic admission, rejects the old post-completion trace, independently reads the actual closed payload and both private move spends, and requires positive absence of the external marker and private completion. Cold inspection must return one unresolved attempt, no success receipt and exact-ID repetition refusal without opening Room or a provider. Both earlier completed backups remain protected, and no app-process restart requires an intervening guest restart. The missing Android bridge reproduced at instrumentation compilation; independent review and native evidence remain pending.

Independent Android boundary review required an inert non-exported debug picker host, no automatic force-stop of unexpected successor processes, and a final result fenced by both earlier backups' independent before/after checks even on APK or fixture setup failure. The corrected runner checks private catalog ownership via both hard links, binds the grant-inventory digest across the real process death, and accepts a matching spent handoff for readback only if the recorded cold test never started and the allocator identifies the immediate predecessor. The host validates spend bytes and their payload bindings, while the live writer—not a shell mount or recorded inode alone—checks provider descriptors. Host regressions cover swapped checkpoint kinds, absent grant fingerprints, attempted handoff after a cold launch, and refusal to seal a passing outcome when either earlier backup changed.

`dea8dca` reached the Android pre-marker death checkpoint with exact operation and payload bindings; Android reported the writer crashed and the cold gate then saw a different package-matching PID. Both older backup preservation checks passed. The first read-only handoff authenticated the spent writer and installed pair, but an exact checksum of the earlier backup's parent fixture timed out before any instrumentation, installation, new staging, or force-stop. Preserve both receipts. Admit one bounded read-only continuation from that exact released timeout claim with the original spent writer trace; retry only the identical checksum on timeout, once, never on a mismatched result. No writer replay or fresh synthetic destination. Cold recovery and final interrupted-payload readback remain unverified.

The [actual pre-marker process-death witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#process-death-before-the-completion-marker) passed through the `dea8dca` writer and `f0f7bd5` read-only continuation. The verified payloads and private spends were retained with no marker or completion; cold inspection showed exactly one unresolved attempt and refused replay. Both earlier completed backups and the original grant inventory stayed unchanged. This closes the exact before-marker process-death witness only. Next highest-value dependency for live portability is proving two independently selected roots under the existing provider identity and activation contracts, without granting write or retirement based on a read observation alone. A full Settings low-storage/cleanup journey remains open independently.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, independent Files roots from `3c40fb2`: test two independently selected, byte-identical populated synthetic sibling libraries, then separately select their synthetic common parent as the descriptor anchor. Use distinct actual chooser results, persist only their exact read grants, and run the cold topology witness on the same exclusively claimed VM with no effective write grant. Verify both portable catalog copies and that distinct root descriptors are separated while the same root reopened as a second descriptor is rejected; reopen exact locators and revalidate the held identities. Preserve the existing grant inventory and all retained folders. Scope is debug/instrumentation/runner and fixture evidence only; the extra anchor selection is a test control, not a new user-facing permission requirement. A later writer must still establish stable topology ownership throughout publication. Recheck after compile/review or within 30 active minutes.

Independent review found four Important gaps in the witness: grant cleanup could stop early or be skipped by cold admission; catalog verification did not hold the same directory descriptors; the pinned predecessor check ran after boot; and earlier backups were not checked across setup. Refuse broad storage permissions in either APK. Revalidate both exact direct ancestry edges under the held descriptors before and after catalog reads and reopen the exact locators. Check the released guest's exact generation under the allocator before claim or boot. Attempt each exact read release independently, retain unexpectedly changed modes, and record unresolved cleanup if the cold witness does not finish. Bind both earlier completed backups, the interrupted attempt, and the capacity fixture to pinned before/after closure evidence; do not seal a result before those postchecks. A refused chooser only backs out of its own verified system window. Missing Kotlin and host guard entry points reproduced at compilation/test discovery. No native run yet; this remains a read-only topology witness.

The first `69d3a12` native run stopped at the first Files confirmation guard, before any grant handoff or cold probe. On API 28 the chooser exposed the exact current title, its three expected managed directories and a valid affirmative action, but did not expose the separate parent breadcrumb required by the fixture. The retained-folder and backup preservation checks passed; the owned guest exited and released. Use an exact nonce in both new sibling titles, admit either the exact breadcrumb or its observed platform header outside the directory list for this named test only, and still require each exact returned tree URI before any persistence. Permit only the pinned immediate predecessor with the unchanged app APK. Confirm the older attempt has no retained fixture grants or handoff record and leave both of its folders unchanged. An unconfirmed picker or cold gate still proves nothing about ancestry.

The `9cdb8a0` grant stage passed all three actual Files selections, proved absent grants on the first attempt's fixture, and durably recorded the three distinct exact read grants and unchanged unrelated-grant digest. The cold stage did not start: a different package-matching PID was left alone, and the runner shut down and released the exact owned guest. Preserve both fixtures and the nine verified receipt anchors. The [independent-root read-only API 28 witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#independently-selected-sibling-libraries) passed through `9cdb8a0` and `b86fb8b`. The fixture read grants were released without changing unrelated permissions, and the earlier backup and fixture anchors verified unchanged. Do not treat the separately picked test-only anchor as production grant authority. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, inferred anchor from `9915054`: compute only bounded parent-hop counts from the two admitted Android document IDs; do not derive, open or request a parent URI. Compare the actual mount/device/inode of both arrived descriptors, then use the existing kernel to recheck both held ancestry chains. An observed common ancestor is not writer authority. The current process lane serializes cooperating provider attempts only, and entering the ordinary reader from inside that lane would try to reacquire it. The first offline candidate passed 1,057 data JVM tests and compiled both Android test suites. The [two-grant Files witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#independently-selected-sibling-libraries) passed on the exact `ac24c38` development pair. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, cooperative attempt ownership from `563ddaf`: reuse the existing non-reentrant provider slot and give a single compound caller an exact, cancellation-aware in-process owner. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, scoped two-root read from `35b8176`: reuse the ordinary provider backend and catalog verifier for both exact trees under one provider owner and cancellation signal. The [shared-owner native reader](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#independently-selected-sibling-libraries) passed on `519401b` via the two actual child Files grants. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, paired candidate comparison from `24300b6`: reuse the catalog planner and missing-object comparison under the same scoped Files reader. Independent review confirmed the byte-comparison and missing-only behavior, but identified a possible merged inventory above the ordinary reader's 8 GiB limit and unbounded retained verification copies under cleanup failure. Bound the union by the same distinct-original admission and budget comparison reads separately. Place both catalog copies under a four-attempt app-private scratch budget; return no estimate on uncertain cleanup and leave unknown files intact. The paired fixture now re-admits both exact URIs and complete generation records from the preceding read. This checks continuity between phases, not exclusion of external modifications or atomic observation of all original bytes.

The first post-review JVM run reported one test assertion mismatch: the admit helper returned the correct ordered objects as a map-values view, while the test compared it directly to a value-equal List. The assertion now compares its contents and keeps the over-budget and conflicting-length refusals unchanged. No provider operation or emulator launch took place for this correction.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, closed pair dry run from `27ae59c`: the corrected comparison suite passed all 1,064 data JVM cases and both Android suites compiled. Build and close one private proposed Room catalog; independently reopen and compare it to the verified metadata and original-object union. Budget actual closed metadata and missing originals separately, then recheck both exact issued inputs under the existing reader. Refuse uncertainty in private cleanup or current authority. Scope: one read-only `Room` preflight and combined two-selection witness, no provider write, active-pointer change, or removal. Recheck at the strict offline build and independent review within 30 active minutes. The next native run must use the exact successor of the shared-owner test, never the old predecessor.

Independent review found two Important gaps: a canceled, definitively closed private candidate could exhaust the bounded scratch budget, and an unreferenced destination object could disappear between comparison and the capacity measurement without a new catalog marker. Capture the exact Room-acknowledged closed stage and retire it on ordinary unwind only under unchanged attempt/file identity and exact inventory. Recompute the match, missing-byte count and capacity under the same final re-admitted pair, rather than carry an earlier missing-byte estimate into the last check. Keep the new host control for removing an unreferenced object and the actual Android control canceling at the closed stage. Neither final read-only check reserves bytes or excludes external changes after return.

The [closed merge dry run](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#closed-merge-dry-run-on-two-selected-folders) passed on the `7647261` pair. Two distinct Files child selections led to a reopened private candidate containing the shared original and both recipe histories. Missing bytes were zero for this identical fixture, usable capacity was checked, and a separate post-close canceled attempt reclaimed its candidate. The previous fixtures, backups and grants passed exact preservation checks. Next build a qualified selected-root publication that consumes the verified predecessor and writes only missing objects and a new generation, with an explicit activation and no source retirement. Native nonzero-copy, real Settings Import/Move, selected graph cutover/reconnect, source retirement and the actual Settings capacity-refusal recovery remain open.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, common immutable append from `1ce3d66`: extract the existing verified local merge commit's marker-last pipeline for the future selected writer to consume. The caller must already own the exact roots, journaled inputs and verified stage. Recheck both input tips after destination payload barriers and immediately before creating a marker; a stale source may leave verified unreferenced payloads, but cannot create a visible successor. Keep read-only reconciliation and prior generations unchanged. Scope is the shared Kotlin publisher, existing private consumer and deterministic filesystem tests. No provider writer, activation, grant, or retirement is introduced. Check at the focused RED and strict offline GREEN within 30 active minutes.

Independent review identified three Important gaps in the first extraction. The local marker writer does additional staging and barriers after its entry check. Pass the final input and destination-payload proof to its actual exclusive-link boundary. Recheck the published catalog and originals after marker sync, even on read-only replay, and thread the Room operation's exact physical location check into the common guard. Reproduce changes during both marker staging and directory barriers; distinguish withholding a marker from refusing to acknowledge a marker already visible. The cooperative ownership prerequisite remains in force.

Four filesystem controls reproduced the failure boundaries before correction: a changed source during payload sync, a changed source during marker staging, a corrupted child catalog during payload sync, and a changed original during replay's marker sync. The corrected run passed all 1,069 data JVM tests and compiled both Android test suites. No provider write was enabled or run. The next consumer needs only the existing trusted local provider, its exact writable destination and read-only source, and a live operation owner; do not add a second publication engine or a generic path-conversion fallback.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, common provider leaf movement from `e9ce0a9`: expose an explicit visibility check after the same attempt's durable one-shot spend and before calling `moveDocument`; recovery never enters that write boundary. Reuse the existing same-basename closed-file provider protocol rather than fork its stage/identity/durability logic. Recheck at strict-offline RED/GREEN and independent review, before enabling any new mutation.

The missing visibility callback reproduced at test compilation. Independent review found no live production regression and identified three missing controls, now covered: a final-name collision during the spend withholds the move, cancellation when the guard returns withholds it, and successful identity-verified reconciliation does not call the guard again. The final offline run recorded 1,993 JVM cases without failures or skips and compiled both Android test suites. The existing export does not supply the new guard; no device was used for this increment. The guarded path still assumes cooperative ownership, and neither the callback nor separate provider lookups establish an atomic no-overwrite guarantee against another process.

For the next native selected-root write, the synthetic fixture generator now has a populated alternate source with the same album title, a different logical scan name and independent scan, source and blob IDs. Actual SQLite and SHA-256 checks distinguish its one missing destination original from the unrelated unreferenced object already present on both sides. Both two-revision histories are independently checked. The missing fixture mode reproduced first; 273 host storage checks passed afterward. This fixture does not itself run a provider or enable writing.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, selected-root immutable append from `c92c1b8`: prove one genuine Files copy between distinct synthetic siblings, with only the destination's exact persisted read/write grant and the source's exact persisted read grant, neither with a parent grant. Reuse the shared paired reader/merge publication, the closed Room projection and the same-basename provider move. A new operation binds both exact roots and tips plus one closed stage before writing, verifies the actual child and retains the predecessor. Refuse cold write replay; preserve unrelated names and the entire incoming folder. Scope is data library composition, the debug bridge, the test-only Files journey and exact-pair host runner; no Settings, active change or retirement. Current check: observe missing debug append at offline compile, then implement and review within 30 active minutes.

The first append candidate compiled and passed 1,073 data JVM cases. Independent review identified occupied child-catalog adoption, missing projected namespace caps, and lack of physical parent checks for the directories that would be mutated. The corrected build requires an absent catalog and a fresh reserved merge staging namespace before mutation, verifies each actual managed directory's physical parent, and checks the projected inventory against ordinary reader bounds. The destination alone uses the explicit writable picker; the source is only read, regardless of temporary platform capabilities. The admission regressions reproduced at compilation, then strict offline passed 1,075 data JVM and 275 host storage cases; both exact development APKs were assembled and inspected. The runner requires one append, independently reopens the pulled destination catalog and verifies its order and four recipe revisions, checks the entire source and preserved predecessor/unrelated bytes, and never replays on failure. This is a one-attempt qualification; it intentionally refuses repeat writes pending durable selected-append settlement. Large-library performance and external-writer exclusion are not established.

Native admission is blocked before any launch or package change: the read-only allocator preflight found a newer exclusive owner for unrelated work. The closed dry-run receipt and its linked predecessor chain authenticate offline, but no longer match the guest's immediate live claim. Do not stop the unrelated test, use a different guest, or weaken the predecessor guard. Actual selected-root publication, selected graph cutover/reconnect, real Settings Import/Move and source retirement remain open.

The intervening owner released the same guest. This admission recognizes only one explicitly named, completed, stopped-and-released generation under the shared allocation lock and requires the original dry-run generation and exact exit proof directly behind it. Keep registration, verified process exit, RAM/disk, installed dry-run pair, private-attempt absence and retained-fixture checks independent. Independent review identified a staging gap after package replacement; the guarded first fixture creation now requires fresh target, installed pair, process, private-attempt and fixture checks. The actual released allocator record matched the richer monitored handoff format; a legacy record without that proof still refuses. All 277 host storage, 47 provider and nine shutdown-fence tests passed. No other runner path gains reassignment authority, and no release record is edited outside the shared allocator. The source and test build inputs are unchanged from the packaged append candidate.

Nonblocking host-runner debt: the optional full decode-runner discovery had ten missing-path errors in 37 tests because two historical runners retain their original workspace root. Rebinding only that root in memory reached all 37 and exposed three stale exact-case inventory assertions in the composition, routing and library-lifecycle suites. Revisit when those historical runners need reuse or before claiming full decode-runner coverage; do not edit the hash-pinned allocator to work around a historical test path.

The first selected-append instrumented attempt on `278ac7f` failed before the first Files launch. The [detached selected-root append](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#first-verified-write-between-two-selected-folders) passed on the `14b611d` development pair. Reuse the merge journal; bind completion to the exact immutable intent and its staged catalog, and let cold inspection distinguish intent-only from a durably reverified completion without constructing a writer. A next synthetic native run must independently preserve that older private attempt and both fixture trees rather than deleting them, forging its completion, or rerunning its write.

Independent review found no Critical issue and two Important private-evidence gaps: retained pre-intent evidence looked absent, and an exact-byte file replaced after directory sync could be mistaken for the synced file. Four regressions reproduced those gaps and the associated sidecar and pre-force extent failures. Cold readback now distinguishes positive absence, refuses all three SQLite sidecars, rechecks the attempt and each exact node across the barrier, admits extent before forcing, and preserves unresolved files. The before-link authority-refusal control also passed. Final strict-offline verification recorded 2,005 JVM cases with no failures or skips, including fourteen merge-journal cases, and compiled both Android instrumentation suites. This is a local receipt boundary, not an observed native close failure, process-death witness or repeat-write result. The allocator's retained guest record still matches the exact verified prior release; there was no guest launch, installation, removal, or publication.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-REPEAT-ADMISSION` from `e6decdc`: serialize one selected append from fresh-ID admission through final receipt, and allow a newly issued ID only when prior attempts are valid, completed, and within the retained scratch bound. Refuse same-ID replay, uncertain prior evidence and unknown namespaces; retain all prior intent, catalogs and spends. Use the existing app-private lock pattern and receipt verifier. Scope is the data library append allocation/lock and host tests; no cleanup, acknowledgment/archive, activation or actual Files operation in this block. Check at RED/GREEN and independent review within 30 active minutes. The next native witness must demonstrate two writes in an isolated synthetic attempt scope while preserving the pinned earlier one and restoring grants.

Repeat-admission review found two Important cases: an unknown private leaf hidden by a valid receipt, and late authority loss during a retained-receipt sync consuming a fresh slot. Both reproduced as JVM failures. The correction requires bounded known leaves, positively identified surviving journal hard links, and fresh authority immediately before every new directory. The strict-offline suite recorded 2,012 JVM cases with no failures or skips (seven selected-allocation cases), compiled both Android instrumentation suites, and passed 278 host storage tests. The four retained attempt limit is intentional until explicit acknowledgment and archival; no evidence is removed. Lock ordering remains statically traced rather than runtime-tested and early refusal does not forbid an already-authorized read-only plan. The provider still refuses a second write to the same destination because its legacy staging directory is deliberately not adopted. A generation-scoped provider stage that leaves older verified attempts untouched is the next prerequisite, followed by the actual two-write LDPlayer witness. This is not a native pass or Settings acceptance.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-STAGE-NAMESPACE` from `d107281`: create a fresh exact stage name under the held destination for the new generation ID only. Never borrow, modify or remove earlier staging directories. Refuse the unbound legacy name, a collision, a stage not named by the verified marker chain, wrong physical containment or residual files. Scope is provider admission, the existing append writer/host oracle and focused storage tests; no guest mutation in this increment. Check at RED/GREEN and independent review within 30 active minutes.

Native continuation admission: use the already established instrumentation-only `ContextWrapper` private-files pattern with an exclusively created synthetic scope; keep the prior default `files/library-selected-merge-v1` journal, both earlier fixture trees and the exact prior grants unchanged. Reuse the same held guest only through its pinned last completed release. Two new confirmed operation IDs must append to one fresh destination, not replay the same ID. The second can use the same incoming library deliberately to prove zero new object bytes and no new scan slots; independently verify both successive catalogs, the incoming recipe-history bindings, their separate empty provider stages and matching private receipts. Process death and lost delivery are distinct subsequent checks.

Independent staging review found two Important gaps. A provider-reported stage could resolve to a different direct child, and an empty provider cursor could omit a physical file. Use a no-follow literal lookup relative to the held root, compare device/inode/mount, keep and compare the distinct retained observations, and require physical emptiness through the held stage descriptor. Before creating the exact new stage, require physical name absence; rebind at file staging and visibility. The Android regressions cover wrong sibling descriptors, symlink aliases, occupied names and an omitted dotfile; missing proof methods reproduced at instrumentation compilation. The initial implementation also confirmed that Android's public `OsConstants` does not expose `O_DIRECTORY`; use a directory-type precheck, a no-follow/nonblocking open and post-open directory identity instead of a hardcoded platform flag. No Android runtime result has been recorded for this proof.

After the review corrections, strict offline recorded 2,014 JVM cases with no failures or skips and compiled both Android instrumentation suites. The host runner's generation-specific stage oracle passed the 278-case host storage suite. The next decisive check is real provider support for the new descriptor proofs and two verified appends on the same synthetic destination, followed by a separate app-process interruption witness; neither is inferred from compilation.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-REPEAT-FILES-WITNESS` from `132388c`: prepare one isolated app-test Files journey that appends twice to the same fresh synthetic folder, compares the first stage identity across the second write, and reopens both private receipts after restoring the prior persisted grants. Scope is the app instrumentation case and exact retained-guest runner/oracle; runtime admission still needs the pinned installed pair and positive preservation of the earlier successful fixture and private attempt. Run on the already assigned released guest only after the host oracle and artifacts verify. Check at integration compilation and independent review, within 30 active minutes.

The two-append instrumentation case compiled. The second dry run requires one shared original and zero missing bytes; the final independent catalog oracle checks the exact two scans in order, the four imported-page revisions and both parent relationships rather than accepting duplicate slots or a missing revision. The absent repeated-oracle entry reproduced before implementation; 278 host storage tests passed. The run is not yet admitted: connect the exact successful predecessor, protected legacy bytes, and both new receipts to a standalone immutable runner mode before installing or writing.

The host marker/receipt oracle now rejects mismatched intent bytes, either root identity or any of the three marker records. For an ordinary new append, pull and verify the private stage digest independently of the external catalog and retain separate exact intent and completion fingerprints. The missing oracle reproduced at host import before implementation; 279 host storage tests passed. The same proof must cover both stages in the dedicated repeat runner. Exact retained-guest admission is next; no guest has been launched or changed.

The standalone repeat mode pins the successful detached-append manifest and both installed predecessor APKs rather than admitting a failed or reassigned owner. It independently snapshots the legacy private attempt and verifies both retained external trees before installing; all checks rerun before the new fixture and at wrap-up. With one named instrumentation case, the host requires two exact successive generations, both closed catalog readbacks and private receipts, three move spends for the first write and two for the zero-copy repeat. Mode-selection tests reproduced the missing gate; 280 storage checks and exact offline authentication of the prior released guest record passed. Independent runner review and exact development artifact admission remain pending; no install, chooser or guest write has occurred.

The locally signed development app and test APKs were assembled strictly offline from the unchanged application and instrumentation inputs of `1ade974`. The SDK checks passed package, signature, instrumentation target, alignment and merged permission boundaries; the APKs contain the descriptor-proof methods and named two-write case. Runner review is still the admission gate. The guest was not used during assembly or SDK verification.

An offline invocation of the exact repeat runner authenticated the whole predecessor and protected-evidence chain with the built pair. An intercepted write boundary stopped it before retaining an APK or creating a fixture, and the allocator was disabled for this diagnostic. This proves the offline admission path, not package replacement, provider access, or native execution.

Independent runner review found three Important gaps: incomplete allocator release records, a grant baseline taken only after installation, and spend names accepted without validating the authorized payload. The added tests reproduced the missing gates. The repeat now requires the exact stopped generation's completed cleanup and no consumers, compares a bounded package-scoped persisted-grant baseline before installation, before fixture staging and on wrap-up, and validates each spend's key, extent and exact payload digest. The protected legacy fixture also requires its exact parent inventory. The [Android 9 activity manager](https://android.googlesource.com/platform/frameworks/base/+/android-9.0.0_r1/services/core/java/com/android/server/am/ActivityManagerService.java) passes the package filter in the full `-a -p` dump, not the direct `permissions` subcommand; extract only the grant section on the guest and refuse a malformed or unscoped result. Host storage passed 283 cases; the exact predecessor record passed the strengthened release gate offline. Actual package-scoped grant access, provider descriptor access and both appends still need native evidence.

The [native repeat check](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#two-verified-writes-to-the-same-selected-folder) passed on the exact `07c47cd` development pair. Both writes targeted the same Files-selected folder; one original was copied on the first write and none on the second. Both catalogs and receipts verified independently, the first provider stage and all older protected evidence remained unchanged, the package-scoped grant inventory matched across installation and after testing, and the guest shut down and released. Use this as the latest native predecessor, preserving both newly completed operations. Next: actual selected-append interruption after completion, no cold replay, then editable selected graph and real Settings journeys. A fresh private reader in the passing test is not a new app process.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-APPEND-DELIVERY-DEATH` from `6f3303a`: prepare one isolated real Files append whose test-process lifetime ends after the closed private receipt and grant restoration, before success is delivered. A separately started headless app process must reopen the exact operation without Room, provider or chooser access and reject use of the old ID before admitting reads or writes. Preserve the exact repeat witness and earlier test data. Scope is the internal per-call observation, private replay guard, debug adapter and test/runner; no same-ID recovery write, no Settings activation or source retirement. Recheck at strict-offline compilation and independent review within 30 active minutes.

The app test initially failed compilation at the missing write-delivery observation and replay entry; the host tests reproduced missing crash classification, process-absence and prior-evidence gates. Independent review confirmed the receipt is after provider and writer-lane closure. It found three Important gaps: an empty `pidof` response did not prove absence, the cold test bypassed the actual append entry, and three retained baselines could be adopted without reference to pinned evidence. The native runner now requires a positive process inventory with neither package nor the reported writer PID, verifies the pre-install grant digest, legacy private checksums and all five prior repeat spends against the exact successful manifest, and arms one exclusive host admission before launching the writer. The headless case uses the real append entry with the prior exact markers and a context that rejects provider and Room access; the spent ID must fail before those accesses. A distinct pre-marker interruption remains open and must not infer a receipt from provider visibility.

The exact `b7f9f63` development pair reached the intended writer checkpoint and returned Android's process-crash result. The immediately following cold admission returned its catch-all refusal, with no indication of whether the shell inventory was incompatible or a process was visible. No headless case, force-stop, new write or replay followed; both guest processes exited and the allocator verified release. The exact attempt remains spent. The read-only continuation binds that one checkpoint and failed admission to the already-installed pair and the immediate released claim, with no package replacement, new fixture or writer branch. It reports only bounded reasons for a process-inventory refusal. A later admissible process after guest restart cannot prove that immediate in-guest cold admission would have succeeded.

The [read-only recovery](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#selected-append-recovery-after-process-death) passed after restarting only the released guest, without reinstalling its exact `b7f9f63` pair. Independent host verification matched the 536,576-byte catalog, original, all three spend digests, private node identities and unchanged protected predecessor evidence and grants. The guest again exited and released. This is the latest native release; retain the interrupted append, both repeat generations and the earlier legacy attempt. Immediate same-guest admission, selected death before marker and editable selected Settings flows remain open.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-EDITOR-READ` from `28c1ad8`: extend the existing selected working Room scope with an activation-issued saved-page editor read. The selected-folder case failed compilation at the missing editor-read entry. Independent read-scope review found no blocking issue. The integration case now also requires the literal second-revision recipe digest from the independently generated host fixture; its final instrumentation compilation passed. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-EDITOR-CANDIDATE` from `6bec15c`: stage one saved-page recipe change using the existing metadata transaction on an independently owned working catalog, not the live folder. The already installed SQLite text16 API reproduced stripping a leading BOM, and an in-memory backup of the exact retained closed fixture showed the old OCR row surviving a valid recipe revision advance with unequal provenance. The added Room cases cover two-page OCR, a leading-BOM title and actual stale revision; they compile but have not run on LDPlayer. No schema or dependency changed. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-EDITOR-CATALOG-PUBLICATION` from `b4016bd`: reuse the shared marker-last publisher for an exact single-root metadata-only successor. The file-backed regressions reproduced the missing catalog-only entry at compilation. Only cooperating owners are serialized; provider write/rebind, full private completion, active-binding adoption and native confirmation remain open.

The final exact-capacity regression failed before the lower-bound check accounted for the serialized spend and marker, then passed. The first exact `d58dac1` candidate never reached Android package admission: the allocator's index-scoped serial probe returned 10 not-found and 55 offline responses inside the boot deadline. Both players/VM processes are verified stopped for their respective claims; neither run consumed the edit authorization. No third launch is planned. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, attempt `SELECTED-SUCCESSOR-POINTER` from `bbf4886`: continue the host-only private pointer prerequisite while Android Files qualification is blocked. The missing advancement entry reproduced at Kotlin compilation. Independent review then exposed a late revocation window: an app-private sync ran after the live authority check. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, `SELECTED-EDITOR-FRESH-GUEST` from `0ff55dd`: the operator authorized necessary guest creation after the two boot-only refusals. The [fresh guest witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#one-folder-recipe-edit) passed on `7578d63`: one real writable Files result, zero original copies, exact original and predecessor identities, one independently reopened successor catalog, exact accepted geometry and Undo pointers/pins, persisted grants restored to empty, and spent-ID refusal before provider access. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, `SELECTED-EDITOR-LIVE-RECEIPT` from `fb309f6`: add the private receipt boundary needed to connect the qualified provider edit to an active-generation update. The application save and active-pointer adoption remain separate. Recheck on RED/GREEN and independent review within 30 active minutes.

The missing journal failed at Kotlin compilation, then four file-backed cases passed. Independent review reproduced two more REDs: a distinct inode with identical completion bytes passed the directory barrier, and an unlinked completion spill looked like fresh pending evidence. The corrected writer binds the forced inode to the final record, checks the bounded attempt/catalog/record identities across inspection, and refuses unexplained spills without removing them. The initial correction exposed that this Windows JDK returns no node key; the common code now requires an explicit identity port, with the same synthetic Windows-test projection used by the selected-merge suite and an independent actual hard-link identity check. `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, `SELECTED-EDITOR-RECEIPT-CONSUMER` from `7dd1f8b`: route the durable active selection and issuing activation to the qualified Files writer. Make the journal intent before any provider stage; record completion only after close, independent readback, current-grant validation and release of the provider lane. Scope excludes the subsequent activation transition, editor success delivery, and retention cleanup. Check at test compilation, independent boundary review and one exact native result, or within 30 active minutes.

The new Android witness first failed at compilation for the missing bound entry. Independent review found a check-to-use gap before provider admission and inconsistent exact write-grant loss classification. The corrected path takes the selected write lease before rechecking the active pointer and spent ID and before obtaining the provider; it keeps that lease until the receipt is forced. Only exact access loss maps to reconnect, while an obsolete activation is not retryable. The instrumentation forbids provider access on both stale and spent requests and for the separate receipt reopen. The independent host receipt oracle rejects a changed activation or catalog even when the substituted record has a matching completion hash. Native late revocation/readback failure, full pointer adoption and editor delivery remain separate from this witness.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, `BOUND-EDIT-EXACT-RELEASE` from `3d67bfe`: the first bound run refused before claim, launch, installation or spend. Its generic repeat gate requires an explicit empty `consumers` roster; the synchronous detached predecessor's allocator record and its pinned pre-release snapshot both predate that field. Do not edit shared state to fill the missing roster or relax other repeat gates. A different record, unknown evidence, or any actual consumer remains a refusal. The new host regression and all 297 storage cases pass. Recheck on the exact bound witness; keep activation and Settings acceptance open.

The reused API 28 guest ran the exact bound receipt instrumentation once and passed it. The host stopped after publication: the oracle incorrectly assumed the `primary:` form, whereas Files returned the exact `home:` form for the same expected child of Documents. Correct the host check to accept the two canonical URI spellings only, without changing the stored grant or normalizing it at runtime. The retained transcript authenticated the untouched active pointer, exact grant, intent, completion, catalog, original bytes, spend and stopped/released guest. The host did not reach the final exact grant inventory, hard-link-owner check, move-spend readback or detached-predecessor postcheck; complete those by inspection only. Never replay the spent edit to make its runner exit green. Native pointer advancement and live editor delivery are separate witnesses.

The [read-only continuation](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#bound-recipe-receipt) authenticated the sole spent writer, exact installed pair, empty grant inventory, unchanged predecessor and earlier detached witness, hard-linked private catalog owner, and both move spends. It neither launched the app and picker nor installed, replayed, or advanced a library. The exact guest is stopped and released again. Next: active-pointer advancement, with the writer lease retained through receipt consumption and read-only reconciliation of an uncertain private swap.

`LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`, `LIVE-SELECTED-GENERATION-ADVANCE` from the bound readback: adopt a direct catalog-only successor while the same exact live receipt, writer lease, provider root and persisted grant are still valid. Leave the previous verification in place and handle an unacknowledged swap through exact readback, never provider replay. Verify host identity and interrupted acknowledgment, both Android compilations, and the actual Files-selected edit followed by fresh startup and old-activation refusal on the same released guest. This is not foreground editor success. Recheck once the independently reviewed host/kernel changes and native witness are available, no later than the next 30-minute active checkpoint.

Course check: a different active task already claimed the eligible app guest after the successful readback release. Preserve that claim and all retained guest evidence. The prior app guest is disqualified by its two scoped transport failures. Continue host work and use a uniquely identified fresh app guest only if the current release cannot be reconciled and all installed-image, exact APK, allocator, RAM and disk gates permit it. A new witness can verify its own native advancement but cannot certify preservation on an uninspected guest.

The intervening task has now stopped and released the eligible API 28 guest. The exact live release, retained immediate history, and original pinned shutdown snapshot agree after accounting for the allocator's projected history fields. No guest access or package check followed this offline reconciliation. Require the exact installed app/test predecessor and preserved bound and detached evidence before the first advancing write; do not accept live or later claims or reuse a spent write. The fresh alternative remains below the 16 GiB floor. Keep the advancement native witness independent of the unqualified app Settings cutover.

The independent app cutover review identified seven Important findings: unreachable selected editor and original preview, overlapping Start empty destination and unbounded-in-time retention stop, undelivered saves, post-return sharing and backup retention regressions, raw default graph consumers, selected browse contention and lost-access routing, and provisional grant lifetime. The two search regressions reproduced in the app JVM suite and passed in the focused rerun: unsupported default-only page search does not run under a selected activation, and lost access clears the results and requests Reconnect. Do not expose unfinished Settings in a deliverable build. Native end-to-end Settings, writable selected acquisition, Cutover/return, selected and default library transfers, and verified source retirement remain active acceptance work.

The local cutover foundation keeps ordinary Default Settings on the verified backup screen; it does not offer an unfinished switch. Strict offline recorded 1,118 data and 299 app JVM cases with zero failures or skips, both Android test compilations, and both local debug assemblies. The host storage suite passed 300 checks. The isolated advance runner on that exact build stopped before claim: another active test had reclaimed the preferred guest after the read-only admission. The retained attempt has neither a claim nor a new operation spend. Preserve the candidate and earlier guest evidence, and reconsider native admission only after a new exact release is positively reconciled or fresh provisioning fits the 16 GiB floor. Host priority is fencing outstanding commands and prohibiting any potentially overlapping write rather than broadening exposure.

The old-create-result regression reproduced an album selection in the successor despite the result belonging to the previous activation. Create, rename and reservation now borrow the issuing default graph and discard stale completions; the compatibility fixture for a still-running reservation also passed without releasing admission on navigation. Strict-offline app JVM verification passed 300 cases and app Android instrumentation compiled for those changes. This protects the named commands, not every cutover, observer teardown or portable operation.

Start empty now rejects a known ancestor/descendant relationship before creating a private stage. For other selected-root pairs, it holds both root descriptors, proves equal common anchor and disjoint ancestry, and rebinds the exact Files locators through directory creation and final leaf visibility. Both Android instrumentation suites compiled and the data JVM suite passed. The added nested-stage native fixture remains unrun; check for unchanged active pointer and an untouched empty stage, then witness a supported disjoint destination. The current guest's two intervening releases match their complete immediate sequence offline; the runner retains installed-pair and old-fixture tripwires before any new write.

The [exact native advancement witness](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#exact-selected-generation-advancement) passed once. Do not replay it: the operation spent authority and the isolated active pointer advanced. The guest was verified stopped and released. Owner remains this portability worktree; the next core witness is opening a selected saved page through the ordinary grid, rendering its Original/Frame with one authenticated source loan, and adopting a Save without making an earlier activation's pixels or receipts current. Review the route and bound read queue at the next native/test boundary or within 30 active minutes; do not expose the Default Settings cutover yet.

Original display now also accepts the verified scoped decoder without requiring a raw, separately opened source locator. Two focused JVM cases failed on the missing scoped entry, then the image JVM suite passed with one loan, exact half-pixel values, and no surviving pixels or allowance when the reader refuses after decode. The original private lookup path retains its independent binding check. Application/Files wiring and visible Frame/reopen evidence are separate.

The selected album now enables ordinary page taps without enabling selected acquisition, batch or rendered export. The Frame and original-on-load-failure routes use the exact selected activation and catalogued page/source binding. A native fixture with the same original bytes and the guest's actual normalization policy checks grid navigation, exact asymmetric Original pixels, foreign source refusal and post-revocation denial. The application and data JVM checks passed, Android instrumentation compiled and both APKs assembled under strict offline verification; 301 host storage cases passed. The standalone native run must still prove the named route and restore the exact prior grant inventory. It does not expose the Default Settings cutover or qualify foreground Save.

Exact API 28 selected Open RED: the isolated native case failed with `A library working catalog is already open`. The verified release and the new folders, retained advanced-edit evidence, empty grant inventory, and absence of an ordinary active pointer were confirmed. This was a real contention failure, distinct from the earlier runner constructor refusal before claim. The successor queues foreground metadata and pixels ahead of the working-catalog lease, checks recorded write-mode loss even during reads, and keeps selected Save/OCR controls gated while foreground adoption is unfinished. Its new native test overlaps editor metadata and original preview, repeats the grid check and revokes only write before revoking read; the runner verifies the exact prior RED release, retained earlier edits, and distinct sibling contents. It remains an isolated composition witness, not ordinary Settings or a Frame Canvas test.

An independent review of the earlier preview increment found the premature Save exposure, read contention, lost write-mode asymmetry, unsupported-format fallback overclaim and runner coverage gaps. The successor stops short of enabling selected Save or OCR, uses the existing exact-activation read paths through one app queue, and reads against a distinct sibling and the prior protected edit receipts. This still rebuilds a catalog per operation and does not make the selected read a physical-root lifetime lock; those are separate from first-page browsing correctness. Real foreground Save delivery, complete Settings and unsupported-format original access remain unaccepted.

The next exact preview run installed the verified pair and stopped during the first new fixture push, with a 15-second scoped transport timeout. It did not launch instrumentation, open Files or spend a publication. Both emulator processes exited and the claim released. Preserve the uncertain partial fixture untouched. An exact read-only continuation can use the earlier independently verified complete synthetic folder with the same native conversion binding, prove the original app pointer is still absent, and recheck the retained advanced/bound/detached receipts before and after; no repeat push or reset is needed. The same-catalog sibling in this older fixture is not the distinct-sibling witness.

That continuation ran the exact installed `c5bf76b` pair without another installation or push. It passed concurrent metadata/Original access, the clickable grid route and the asymmetric Original pixels, then failed on 56 bytes still charged after the fixture requested grid removal. This equals the display budget of the fixture's rotated 2×3 thumbnail, not its 3×2 Original. The test's polling for zero could return before a not-yet-rendered thumbnail reserved its bitmap. This identifies a plausible timing issue, not proof that production releases every thumbnail; the successor uses the test Activity's stopped lifecycle as an explicit barrier. No write-only or read revocation result was established by this attempt. The complete and partial fixtures, earlier receipts, empty grant inventory and absent ordinary pointer were preserved; both guest processes exited and the claim was released. The host regression rejects a retry that does not bind this exact no-provider-write outcome and installed pair.

The local Default backup history correction admits an older receipt only after verifying continuity with that Default library's retained origin. Discovery and private settlement cannot renew chooser/provider write authority. Another cutover gate under test closes the issuing Default graph outside the canceled caller after a durable leave, retaining a block on opening a new graph if its close remains uncertain. Neither permits source retirement or opens the ordinary Settings cutover.

Independent cutover review found that an unacknowledged close could still admit a selected graph or publish a return to Default; a proof `Error` bypassed invalidation; wrapping the borrow result bypassed the direct graph-escape check. An idle Room observer can reproduce a real close refusal in a consuming fixture. Keep normal Default Settings unchanged: the two JVM cutover controls passed, but they do not cover these review cases or actual Room drain. Correct the admission boundary before exposing any live cutover.

The provisional grant review confirmed the pointer hook is after the last destination and cancellation check, but found a second-owner race: a new preview could reuse another preview's temporary modes, activate the folder, then lose its grant when the stale first preview closed. Serialize provisional ownership by exact URI until transfer or verified-scope cleanup; do not use URI normalization or revoke modes not introduced by that owner. Pending-review disposal and cancellation before cleanup dispatch need their own controls.

The two cutover review regressions reproduced before correction: an `AssertionError` left the old graph available, and a wrapped result escaped the direct graph guard. Both now pass; the error test checks its type and message rather than assuming reference identity across Kotlin coroutine stack recovery. Selected folder operations and Return to Default now consult the same unacknowledged-close fence. The permission-owner regression reached the missing registration seam first; its successor retains the exact key through release and prevents stale duplicate cleanup from affecting a later claim. Ordinary ViewModel disposal dispatches the same narrow permission cleanup on an application-process scope. Android grant disposal and real Room-drain evidence remain outstanding.

Course check — `LIBRARY-PORTABILITY-01`: the next core witness is one exact successor read against the retained complete synthetic fixture, including a stopped preview host, zero allocation after closure, loss of recorded write and then read, and unchanged originals/receipts. The retest may replace only the reconciled exact APK pair and does not restage either fixture. An unresolved Close after Default departure must block both selected startup and Return to Default; a real retained Room observer is the next cutover failure control once the isolated read passes. Keep the first native selection/Save journey on an isolated synthetic graph until the real Home/editor wiring is exercised. Main advancement, source retirement, and fresh guest provisioning are not eligible from this read slot. Check again on the native result or within 30 active minutes.

The [exact selected preview successor](../../validation/storage/library-folder-android.md#selected-catalog-grid-and-original) passed on `92de002`: concurrent metadata/Original, real grid tap, asymmetric Original pixels, actual Activity STOP, allowance release, and recorded write/read revocation without a Default fallback in the isolated scope. Earlier fixtures, receipts, ordinary pointer absence and grant inventory verified; both emulator processes exited before release. The next primary slice is a consuming editor adoption boundary with a stale-preview and delivery-cancellation regression; a real MainActivity Settings → album → editor → verified Save/reopen remains the decisive witness. With only about 12.38 GiB free at read closeout, remeasure and reserve the next exact APK pair before a retained-guest run; fresh provisioning is below its floor. Do not discard earlier evidence to make space.

Foreground Save remains the next activation boundary after the isolated read witness. Reuse the live writer's exact receipt and recorded successor; capture the post-pointer obligation before dispatch can discard it, invalidate old preview/metadata delivery, and distinguish successful adoption, loss after a verified pointer advance, and an external publication without a confirmed pointer. The last case must not mint a new operation or treat the visible child as an acknowledgment. Keep the existing no-auto-replay and retained Undo/producer checks. For tentative folder grants, release introduced modes on definite pre-pointer refusal/owner disposal only; bind retention to the first exact pointer publication attempt, not to confirmation or private staging.
