No process was terminated and the guard continues to reject independent live Gradle work.

The first strict-offline image run passed 248 JVM tests. Independent review identified that a regular-file check cannot prove the absence of hard links; link ownership and stable ancestry are now explicit prerequisites of the private directory owner, not portable NIO guarantees. The expanded suite reproduced the missing post-I/O cancellation check (one intended failure in six tests, no skips); the separate extent and three symbolic-link controls passed on the current filesystem.

The corrected strict-offline image suite passed 251 JVM cases, including all six verifier tests with no failures or skips. The verifier is not wired to an installer or active native engine. Preserve the private-owner rule when a consuming runtime opens the directory.

`OCR-DIRECT-IMPORT-SIGNAL-34` — coordinator, input `303d08e`. Attach the existing process-owned OCR notification to the non-Atelier library import path, one exact acknowledged child commit at a time. Gallery, file and camera all use the same controller. Keep the Atelier controller's staging imports silent until their existing authenticated Save. Preserve cancellation, mismatch refusal, replay idempotence and ingestion outcomes. Scope: app import callback routing and focused host tests, with no runtime packaging or device mutation.

The focused build reproduced the missing post-commit callback at the three new call sites. The first implementation exposed an Android-test trailing lambda constructor compatibility issue, corrected while preserving the two-argument constructor used for silent staging and existing callers. The reviewed strict offline build passed 210 app JVM cases and compiled Android tests; real picker/camera and WorkManager delivery did not run. Failed wake dispatch cannot rewrite a successful import or notify for rejected, pending, or mismatched children.

`OCR-RASTER-ADMISSION-35` — coordinator, input `5f5386a`. Choose a bounded, aspect-preserving raster for the qualified Tesseract contract, without lowering export or substituting a different saved recipe. Use the shared full-source identity path and reserve the same application allowance. Oversized or temporarily unavailable OCR input must not change a committed scan or count as a recognition start. Scope: renderer purpose/raster policy, typed preparation, focused tests and this record. Private synthetic evidence cannot close production-size native memory, language fidelity or device acceptance.

Independent review found that the existing floating-point fit requests `1999×2400` for an admissible `2105×2526` target, which the common-scale predicate refuses. The focused control reproduced the mismatch; OCR now floors the exact `Long` rational scale, independently of export. The real coordinator tests reject a 2401-wide OCR request against an equally wide source and reject OCR fallback before decode, then render the admitted 2400-wide request. The corrected strict offline run passed 283 core and 253 image JVM cases. Bounding the destination alone cannot fit a full 12 MP source for OCR under the existing shared reservation; source plus stage pressure and transient retry classification remain separate activation blockers. Neither the sizing test nor the display model establishes production-sized native memory or recognition acceptance.

`OCR-GUARDED-TEXT-36` — coordinator, input `2d1f1e1`. Read a saved page's verbatim OCR only after proving live page and result producer in the same Room transaction. Preserve `OcrText("")` as a completed blank and expose the original producing settings while a same-input rerun proceeds. No plaintext reaches search hits or diagnostics. Scope: read-only DAO/repository surface, focused tests, and receipt; no schema or worker state changes.

The missing read reproduced in host SQL extraction and Android test compilation. Independent review showed that SQLite admits malformed UTF-8 in TEXT storage, so the corrected DAO projects only a length-admitted BLOB and the repository strictly decodes it before `OcrText` validation. The SQL tests now also distinguish the exact fallback byte ceiling from the larger indexed representation. The Android fixtures use different pending settings to test the retained producer rather than today's job. Verification: 172 host storage tests, 798 data JVM tests, and Android test compilation. The two new Android test methods have not run; no package or schema changed.

`OCR-HOME-PAGE-HITS-37` — coordinator, input `ca24bba`. Put recognized-text page matches under the existing Home search alongside, but distinct from, title/date album results. The first strict-offline candidate passed 212 app JVM cases and compiled Android tests. Independent review found two navigation/paging gaps: an in-flight click could outlive a newer tap, Settings, or Capture, and a failed continuation could stop or discard the other stream. The corrections fence each click by query/view/navigation generation, cancel when leaving Home, and preserve independent retry cursors and empty-result semantics. The final strict-offline run passed 214 app JVM cases and Android test compilation. No app APK/guest navigation was exercised. The 16 exact, link-free, repeated-byte generated `SourceStoreTest` limits were NTFS-compressed in place without changing their SHA-256s or touching retained APKs; this recovered 1.46 GiB on the build volume.

`OCR-PRIVATE-EXACT-PACK-38` — coordinator, input `6c8e145`. Use the real installed-pack verifier before private JNI lease acquisition and use guarded verbatim readback after real OCR publication and store reopen. Check byte corruption in a separately created, disposable test pack directory, not the retained stage or actual selected packs. Scope: private image instrumentation and its receipt only. Reuse the installed-stage dev key/LDPlayer path; no app packaging, data deletion, unqualified redistribution or new dependency.

[Private exact-pack and text-readback receipt](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#exact-installed-byte-and-private-text-readback): independent review required proof of refusal before the pack lease and a contemporaneous source-to-APK record. The revised exact probe passed on LDPlayer API 28: 20/20 single-language fixtures exact under declared layouts, 23/24 normalized overall, 48/48 search hit sets, and 24/24 verbatim text and producing-setting comparisons in both publish and reopen phases, including the durable blank. The corrupt-pack control cannot reach lease acquisition, and the subsequently successful English job retained attempt one. Ten evidence anchors and verified shutdown/release accompany the new APK. `OCR-SOURCE-LIFETIME-39` — coordinator, input `0b75616`. For OCR only, end the full decoded-source lease after successful geometry handoff, before appearance. The independent geometry raster keeps its own reservation. Retain full original byte/pixel authentication, the exact accepted recipe, and the existing allowance; do not shrink output, change other render purposes or lower a stage's estimate. Scope: coordinator ownership transition, image JVM tests and shared-contract receipt. Verify an explicit 12 MP-class host-ledger witness and an independent pixel oracle. This does not qualify native memory, temporarily occupied budget handling, or a normal APK.

The `3024×4032 → 1800×2400` identity-appearance host case reproduced `RESOURCE_LIMIT` at a 192 MiB allowance. Releasing the full source before appearance changed the accounting peak for the four-byte stand-in from 223,730,500 to 197,744,964 bytes; the exact case rendered successfully and the literal uniform RGB values and final owner accounting matched. Strict offline verification passed 254 image JVM cases and Android test compilation. Independent review found no Critical or Important issue. The uniform check does not establish spatial/edited-page parity, and the changed OCR-specific failure and canceled-delivery branches lack direct tests; the existing common-path tests remain in place. With this fixed geometry and idle ledger, the source's encoded bytes may occupy at most 3,581,632 bytes; a 4 MiB original already exceeds the remaining allowance. This is not general 12 MP support, device evidence, or a classification of transient contention.

`OCR-RENDER-BUDGET-40` — coordinator, input `147c607`. Reuse the existing decode, geometry and appearance estimates to tell a proven idle-fit/temporarily occupied renderer ledger from an impossible idle fit before OCR recognition starts. Do not promote native allocation failures or proof-work limits into retryable ledger pressure. Scope: OCR-only source decode admission callback, renderer and preparer error mapping, image host tests and receipt. Keep other purposes, the source capability, scheduler request, schema and accepted recipe unchanged. Bitmap/JNI capacity and native performance require separate evidence.

Three initial JUnit REDs covered impossible idle fit before codec decode, temporary headroom before decode, and a reservation lost to another owner after decode. Independent review found that the initial correction over-trusted a matching error key. Three further JUnit REDs reproduced false retries from an independent decode ledger and two larger actual decode charges: one whose reservation exceeded the idle limit, and one that only fails combined with geometry. The reviewed correction adds narrow typed allocation evidence (the denying ledger and actual requested bytes) under the existing structured failure base, and subtracts this render's actual still-owned reservations before treating denial as contention. The final strict-offline invocation passed 260 image JVM and 214 app JVM cases and compiled image Android tests. The preflight follows committed-byte proof and does not bypass normalized-pixel authentication. Retry eligibility does not guarantee eventual headroom; bitmap/JNI admission, actual WorkManager timing and sustained foreground contention are untested. No schema, dependency, package or device operation.

Recheck same-album and live current binding, state vocabulary and ready producer metadata. `OCR-SAVED-PAGE-DETAILS-42` — coordinator, input `1465d08`. Expose **Processing details** and **OCR text** on the saved-page editor ribbon, separate from the existing Page info and the import wizard. The compiled-panel regression reproduced that dependency, then passed after the dialog moved to plain read-only text. `OCR-EXPLICIT-RERUN-43` — coordinator, input `53614c4`. Add a data-owned explicit rerun command for an observed current job. The missing command and review readback predicate reproduced independently at test compilation. The review found that a late write could preserve the aggregate source count while relocating the predecessor's exact pin, or save the successor as PENDING with a nonzero attempt. Recheck the exact successor row and the predecessor's pin presence and identity inside the transaction. The four Room scenarios, overlapping submit/cancellation, stale visibility classifications, and a cross-store runtime refusal are not yet device evidence. The store's Java surface checks do cover nonconstruction and nonexposure of the binding. A changed ordinary revision produces a conflict; an unavailable page or unsupported replacement may be refused earlier by the existing guarded reader. Implementation follow-up after the fixture invocation is active: `OCR-RERUN-PANEL-47` prepares the app-only consuming controls and host presentation witnesses from `8e5717f`. Missing presentation and app-signal contracts reproduced at host compilation. Initial checks passed 226 app JVM cases. Independent review identified that closing/reopening the panel could bypass its local guard; the added host control reproduced two commits while the first wake was blocked. The correction keeps one manual command in flight per application process and exposes the busy state after reopening; canceling the waiting panel cannot cancel an already committed wake. Operation feedback requests visibility in the existing scroll container and does not claim a recognition start. Final strict offline checks passed 227 app JVM cases and compiled app Android tests. `OCR-PACK-OWNER-48` — coordinator, input `4cd3940`. Add a private owner for one immutable trusted OCR pack catalog. Reuse `VerifiedOcrPackDirectory` for exact ordered and persisted identities. Write new bytes only to an exclusive private staging directory; publish a complete verified set atomically at a catalog-derived path, with no overwrite, repair, or removal of retained bytes. On reopen prove private ancestry and single-link pack ownership independently of digest equality before lending the read-only native directory. Scope: image OCR filesystem code and synthetic JVM witnesses. No real pack installation, actual runtime activation, dependency, schema, publishing, or device operation.

The missing owner reproduced at host compilation. The initial host candidate passed 263 image JVM cases after using APIs available on the Android compile surface. Independent review found that a caller-selected path could not establish private ancestry and that lending the whole directory after hashing selected files could expose other corrupt pack bytes. The second issue reproduced in the unselected same-length corruption test. The correction binds construction to the real Application's protected package files directory, uses a fixed private child with owner checks and no exposed test constructor, and rechecks every file offered to the engine. A cooperating process/file lock serializes publication, not arbitrary same-UID hostile writes. The corrected strict offline build passed 265 image and 227 app JVM cases and compiled image Android tests. On-device UID/link proof, actual native path consumption, cross-process contention, directory crash durability and the cost of hashing the entire catalog are not yet qualified. Cancellation after the atomic move does not undo publication; no published set is overwritten or deleted.

`OCR-PACK-OWNER-LD-49` — coordinator, input `cf94740`. Use the existing receipt-gated private image test only. Replace its ad hoc pack staging with the actual Android Application-bound owner and reread the same committed pack catalog from a new owner before native inference. The [Android pack-owner receipt](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#android-pack-owner) records two actual native REDs: Android's app-owned `0771` files-directory mode and the Android NIO provider's unsupported `getFileStore`. Admit the same-app group only for the fixed platform-managed `files` directory and use a path-local usable-space estimate, without relaxing checks on the pack tree. Ten evidence anchors, both original processes exiting and allocator release were verified. A second verified owner reused the catalog without recopy. Independent review found that a running wake could be mislabeled as active recognition, and a transient read could leave a still-open tile stale or silent. Start with metadata already validated at import: actual source format and the effective EXIF orientation applied by decode. Exact `format:jpeg|png|webp` and `orientation:1` through `orientation:8` queries avoid interpreting ordinary OCR words or digits as metadata filters. The first missing user-visible result is an ordinary saved scan becoming searchable offline, with a Home hit opening its exact page; private JNI and Room fixtures do not prove this. The prior block put too much effort into `.txt` internals while this remained blocked. Preserve the verified work, but park extra export plumbing, EXIF fields, and custom provider abstractions. `.txt` Save as remains an acceptance item; return to its shortest safe consuming path after the first ordinary OCR witness. Ordinary scan-to-image/PDF export remains a separate core journey, not a substitute for `.txt`.

- **Gate, next:** Resolve the existing local-only runtime and clean-LDPlayer approvals independently. Reuse the exact qualified Tesseract inputs; one explicitly enabled local candidate first, default builds unchanged. Keep pinned identity, current native diagnostic and resource checks, and no-Internet verification. No new acquisition, production signing, redistribution or publication. If the gate stays closed, stop adding adjacent infrastructure to compensate. No build or guest slot was reserved or consumed by this course check.
- **Core, after both gates:** Coordinator uses the single build/fixture slot. First run one synthetic ordinary import → correct → save → automatic OCR → Home query → exact page in a multi-page album; reopen once. Observe actual saved job, wake, result, and page identity. If this fails, fix the first reproduced blocker. If it passes, exercise the existing-library path, mixed English/French, RTL, CJK, blank, persisted defaults, actual interruption and three-start recovery, then export journeys. Review after the first end-to-end result or within 30 minutes of active work, whichever comes first; a blocked operation consumes no additional test slot while waiting. No unmeasured device envelope becomes accepted.
- **Later, comparator:** The question is whether a small VLM produces materially better literal text and exact-page search for difficult held-out scans without losing supported languages or breaking the target resource/privacy envelope. Use the same saved rasters and ground truth, a pinned prompt/runtime/quantization, and measure missing or invented lines, exact identifiers, blank false positives, query hit precision/recall, cold/warm latency and peak memory. Add a bounded candidate-only comparison, outside the app first and only with separate acquisition/run permission, if the baseline exposes a concrete gap or the operator explicitly prioritizes comparison. Prefer a compact specialist for an ordinary OCR gap, or the vision-only sibling for a layout/handwriting hypothesis. No automatic VLM fallback or replacement of literal results before relevant evidence.

Evidence for prioritization: the [private Tesseract run](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#receipt-gated-runtime-binding) verified 20/20 single-language controls, 23/24 overall and 48/48 hit sets on synthetic material, not ordinary UI, measured handset fit or representative accuracy. The pinned AAR plus packs total 62,856,546 bytes. The [Omni GGUF inventory](https://huggingface.co/api/models/LiquidAI/LFM3-Omni-2B-GGUF?blobs=true) at `d508b88f08a5b4286f2d2805a6a8d69a0ff44c72` lists a 1,099,630,080-byte Q4_K_M model and a 291,586,720-byte Q4_K_M multimodal projector (1,391,216,800 bytes combined, about 22.1× the Tesseract inputs; not RAM, APK delta or a same-precision performance comparison). The [Omni model card](https://huggingface.co/LiquidAI/LFM3-Omni-2B) reports OCRBench 806/1,000; [OCRBench](https://arxiv.org/abs/2305.07895) includes VQA and extraction, not just full-page transcription. Liquid's [documented text list](https://docs.liquid.ai/lfm/models/lfm3-omni-2b) has 16 languages and overlaps 15 of the selected 20. Dutch, Greek, Hebrew, Turkish and Ukrainian are not listed for text; this is a coverage evidence gap, not proof the model cannot read them. [Android `llama.cpp` integration](https://docs.liquid.ai/deployment/on-device/llama-cpp/mobile) exists but the documented mobile examples retain earlier checkpoints and do not certify this exact candidate on PristinePage's target. No weights or runtime were downloaded, no engine selection or authority was changed, and no comparative OCR test was run.

First runtime course check: the exact initial APKs installed on the one new guest. Both pages reached durable READY with one recognition start, and the Room result matched only page two. Corrected [runtime OCR import](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#first-runtime-ocr-import): one actual API 28 test passed with the unchanged app APK and reviewed test-only update. First Files attempt opened New scan and tapped Import photos. The next hierarchy read timed out without bytes; the Files action and a file selection were not observed or performed. The synthetic app draft and fixture were retained, and both guest processes exited and released. The bounded retry reached From files. Android then displayed a repeated-crash dialog naming the instrumentation package; no file was picked. A subsequent read-only boot resolved the normal DocumentsUI handler but found no retained crash stack. The paired APK declares a test-only DocumentsProvider and its DEX lacks the Kotlin runtime that ordinary instrumentation obtains from the target. That is a concrete lead, not a proven cause. Capture the live crash stack on one controlled reproduction before changing the fixture or any app code.

Controlled reproduction confirmed `NoClassDefFoundError` for Kotlin `CollectionsKt` during the test-only `SyntheticDocumentsProvider` static initializer. Android's reader was starting the provider from the test APK alone, not as instrumentation with the target class loader. Ruling: keep the existing fixture and manifest boundary, using only Java and the platform SDK within this provider rather than adding a runtime, changing the app or disabling system picker components. Strict-offline Android test assembly passed; the exact app bytes did not change. The next check is whether DocumentsUI stays open and serves a real selected file with the new test-only package installed.

The rebuilt test provider let the same From files action open DocumentsUI without a crash. Ruling: handle the observed French navigation label in the driver, not by changing the guest locale, and select only the verified synthetic file under its exact unique name. Picker selection and subsequent app actions remain open at this checkpoint.

The next attempt opened Downloads and displayed the verified fixture directory, but DocumentsUI's row delegates its tap to the list and does not advertise `clickable=true`. The saved tree places that exact title in an enabled, focusable direct child of the picker directory list. A subsequent run hit an intermittent null UI root during the activity transition. Ruling: admit only that exact platform row shape for the synthetic filename/folder, keep the app's usual clickable guard unchanged, and reobserve once on an explicit null-root read without replaying input. No partial picker selection or application defect was inferred.

The corrected Files run selected the uniquely named, hash-verified PNG through real DocumentsUI, opened Review pages, used full-frame perspective, selected Natural lightness, saved, observed one saved page in the resulting album and went back to Home. Next check is the exact Home hit and recognized text in the saved page editor; no export or airplane-mode claim yet.

The exact album survived the guest restart. A second external UI attempt confirmed the keyboard opened and dismissed it, but the same hierarchy command failed before a search witness. The continuation must read the persisted accepted correction, wait for actual OCR of this exact saved revision, click the real Home page hit and check editor identity and verbatim OCR display, then create the exact single-page JPEG in private storage. Do not reimport or substitute a seeded OCR response; external Save as remains a separate boundary.

The [actual Files-to-JPEG witness](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#system-files-saved-correction-ocr-and-private-jpeg) passed on LDPlayer API 28. Independent validation-boundary review found three Important pre-action gaps: stopped does not mean radios restored, the Settings Wi-Fi flag does not prove adapter state, and a provider URI checked after writing cannot authorize a destination beforehand. It also found incomplete instrumentation transcripts could be accepted. The cold-boot follow-up proved Wi-Fi was still connected despite `wifi_on=2`, refused to launch the app, then restored and verified the original settings and shut down. The ordinary runner now refuses unattended airplane switching and Save as confirmation rather than weakening their gates. Only a no-confirmation picker inspection is eligible while the exact provider/directory and absent leaf cannot be bound before writing. The next checkpoint is that inspector outcome; do not repeat the live airplane switch or count host parser checks as an offline app test.

`OCR-TEXT-PUBLICATION-100` — after the complete ordinary app journey, consume the existing guarded private text export instead of introducing an OCR or album-export engine. Keep the authorized outward effect one explicit local-document Save as result, not clipboard, cloud synchronization or transmission. Use a separate durable consent record tied to the exact text FileTransaction, its digest/length and an existing process-owner ID. Never create an album artifact or retain text in a Bundle. Validate close/readback, empty text, cancellation/duplicate result, provider refusal, and process-death ambiguity without replaying an external write. Both scopes are required; a private export and a picker launch alone do not complete this work.

Ruling: reuse the closed private artifact, exact transfer primitive, Android document-picker contract and existing positive owner-end proof. Use a separate additive consent table, not a generic second file journal or a pretend album artifact; a foreign key retains the exact private transaction and a unique index prevents another destination from reusing that same consent-bound snapshot. No persistent URI grants or automatic provider replay. Consequence: after death during a provider write, the retained record can honestly report that verification is needed, not reinstate lost authority. Verify migration on a populated test database, and test only synthetic controlled destinations; approval for the previous ordinary JPEG write does not by itself authorize a different real external destination.

The first host migration tests reproduced the missing separate table. The real data test compilation initially found a cold-cache dependency gap; strict replay resolved only the three exact locked data test configurations, then the offline compile reproduced the missing publication facade. After implementation the isolated API 28 self-target APK passed three actual Room cases: populated v1 through v5 migration into v6 preserving existing data and FTS, consent committed before the chooser and exact one-use UTF-8 authorization, and cancellation of a valid empty artifact without a provider write. The application APK was not changed by that run, and both emulator processes exited and released. The ordinary UI and actual local destination remain distinct witnesses.

The ordinary continuation then reproduced the OCR-text `Loading` failure on the exact retained Files scan. A scoped phase trace on a separate APK showed the effect entering while RESUMED, the same-revision read returning and a snapshot being published, but the dialog still showed Loading. The trace ended on test teardown; do not treat teardown as the cause. Read the state from the host composition and pass the resolved value into the dialog, then repeat the original test on the uninstrumented successor. No production phase logs remain.

The first chooser attempt did not reach a destination. Independently, `quit` stopped both original emulator processes but the exact registered index briefly returned under a fallback name with a reset configuration. Using the original retained instance settings and recorded local ADB setting, the same stopped guest's configuration was recovered under the allocator lock with the original disks and other instances untouched. A subsequent diagnostic run booted that exact guest and released both original processes normally. No repeated quit, phone or shared-server access.

Independent text-publication review found four important gaps: unbound system picker, unknown destination on abandonment, refused writes represented as partial, and no exact frozen retry. Bind the documented Android system picker before launch and keep the provider/grant checks; distinguish refusal and permission loss, retain a known target only for private presentation, and let a new explicit consent retry the prior frozen bytes without rewriting the old destination. The v6→v7 migration changes only the file-transaction index to admit more than one separately recorded consent under the same immutable file, while disallowing a second active or already acknowledged save for that snapshot. Retain the earlier v6 schema because a local signed runtime already opened it.

On the exact uninstrumented review successor, the formerly failing saved Files continuation displayed its verbatim OCR text and opened the bound system text chooser. The restarted host build had already completed in its retained log; use those exact bytes rather than inferring it failed from the harness notification.

The reviewed self-target data APK passed five named API 28 Room cases: migrate populated v1–v6 libraries to v7 and reopen the FTS index, commit consent before chooser and fence duplicates, cancel a valid zero-byte preparation, retain the exact frozen bytes after producer replacement, and recover a simulated unacknowledged write without replay. The published destination was not exercised by the simulator. The [scoped Android log observation](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#native-diagnostic-admission-remains-open) found both markers and 210 intervening lines, 88 mentioning either OCR library and none matching the two synthetic text anchors or the two pack-locator patterns. `OCR-NATIVE-INIT-SINKS-109` — scoped qualification, not an application change. Reuse the exact pinned OCR engine self-target instrumentation and the released synthetic LDPlayer pool. The initial exact probe reached and refused native initialization as intended, but its stderr check assumed the canaries were the first and last bytes emitted by every thread in the self-target process. It returned no usable sink count. Require one ordered, unique pair and count only that interval; on failure retain only capture length and marker offsets. The original owned processes exited and its claim was released; manifest `4177c4cc79c70a25783d35e275d3b43aca9aeb1c040a8c85d0af5600439a4c7d`. Do not adjust engine settings to make the probe pass.

The second probe captured 491 decoded characters but neither expected marker, which rules out the original ordering assumption alone. For the final bounded diagnostic, write the control markers directly through the pipe's owned writer and independently match the duplicate descriptor's device/inode to that pipe. This isolates marker routing from the Java standard-stream object. No raw payload is disclosed. The second claim was released after both processes exited (manifest `c609ad24068372844ea6c909c747a51f4bf3c74fc78b01b2de6b75fb87198fd2`).

The [final synthetic initialization probe](../../validation/providers/ocr-tesseract-qualification.md#native-diagnostic-admission-remains-open) verified a missing-pack native refusal and eight nonblank native stderr lines, one matching the synthetic path markers; the separate PID log window had 15 lines and no path-marker matches. No image was submitted for recognition. The exact probe was installed and byte-matched; both owned processes exited and the guest was released. No product bytes or pack data changed. A successful count does not close the privacy/release gate: logcat alone does not cover native stderr, and a production-grade containment approach cannot be inferred from the test-only descriptor redirection.

The successor passed strict-offline debug and unsigned-release assembly, 1,640 JVM tests and 43 provider checks. Both APKs passed offline manifest, exact OCR asset/native inventory, 16 KiB alignment and DEX class checks. The previously working guest's successor attempt stopped at index-scoped `device offline` before any install, fixture staging, or radio mutation; verified shutdown and release followed. Static linkage and full notices remain separate. Keep the `main` merge, separate version bump and push pending.

The alternate released guest passed cold offline Files, automatic recognition, and exact Home reopen on the successor byte-verified pair; one startup backfill and three WorkManager transport/pending-state cases passed. Standard fresh install, device-level unavailable-runtime scanning, native diagnostic containment and complete exact notices remain open. `OCR-RECOVERY-112` — coordinator, input `be4e7e0`. Keep one standard app and the existing exact AAR, catalog and worker. An unavailable runtime must not mark startup reconciliation complete; returning to the scanner after temporary unavailability must automatically discover pages saved meanwhile. Use the foreground start signal and existing process deduplication rather than a periodic worker, a second app variant, or a new persistence lane. Scope: application OCR signals, MainActivity startup, pack-owner lock refusal, focused JVM/device tests and receipts. Do not bypass native diagnostics, redistribution or fresh-install gates and do not publish.

Three host regressions reproduced the no-runtime startup latch, overlapping catalog-lock exception, and a concurrent foreground event lost during a refused pass. Keep one coalesced foreground event behind an in-flight incomplete pass, discard it after success, and do not poll. The independent review also required proof that the original save wake has ended before the Android fixture releases its lock; the test drains the existing process-owned wake scope while the Activity is stopped. Ruling: the fixture demonstrates same-JVM refusal, not a continuous kernel-level lock in the presence of another same-UID process. Existing scheduler start accounting, Room and native payload bytes are unchanged.

The reviewed implementation passed 1,643 JVM cases, 45 provider host checks and 193 storage host checks. Standard debug and unsigned release assembled; the release APK has the expected offline manifest, exact pinned OCR payload and 16 KiB alignment. An earlier runtime candidate completed the Files outage and recovery; the first reviewed APK attempt timed out in the picker, and the next attempt found the resulting unfinished scan before it could reopen Files. Resuming only an empty, nonediting synthetic draft reached DocumentsUI again, where the next attempt refused a control that changed before input. The fixture now waits for consecutive valid observations and dispatches at most one click on that control. No accepted content was discarded, and the allocator verified radio restoration and shutdown after each attempt.

The [reviewed standard recovery candidate](../../validation/providers/ocr-standard-build.md#foreground-recovery-after-temporary-ocr-refusal) passed the decisive same-process API 28 journey in airplane mode: save and exact album-title reopen during pack refusal, wait for the original wake to finish, then recover on foreground and reopen the exact page by recognized text after one actual start. The same exact app/test pair also passed unmodified offline Files and startup backfill. Local recovery evidence does not clear the standard first-install, exact native diagnostics or redistribution/notice gates.

Native release follow-through: a simple `debug_file` redirection is not an acceptable fix. The installed ELF's verified call sequence feeds the `get_debugfp()` pointer to `vfprintf` without a null check; an unsuccessful open of the configured sink would introduce a possible same-process crash. The target wrapper pins NDK r27c, but only r28c is installed and no source tree is retained. Keep the builds local pending explicit source/toolchain scope and exact requalification, and keep a clean standard first launch pending an eligible clean LDPlayer guest. Do not delete old guest data or restart a shared server to satisfy either gate.

Course check: preserve the one-app Files evidence and use only bounded current-build scheduler recovery checks; do not expand the completed 20-image smoke set. Next release decision needs scoped authority for the exact upstream source and a newly qualified runtime, not another run of the retained native logging probe. The latest standard scope excludes creating another guest, and the VM volume was also below the earlier 16 GiB fresh-guest threshold. Resume fresh-install work only with an eligible data-free LDPlayer target and applicable scope. `main`, build version, and publication stay unchanged.

The final boundary review confirmed that the passing recovery test excludes a late save wake. It also identified two distinct cases: a runtime that can rediscover existing jobs but cannot verify defaults must leave new-job backfill eligible, and a failed committed-save wake can require another backfill even after an earlier successful pass. Preserve current-job delivery without treating either as a completed pass. A resumed draft is not proven disposable merely because it is empty; the test now refuses any pre-existing draft without cancelling its pending import. The additional interruption attempt on the earlier reviewed pair stopped at a bounded targeted `pm path` timeout before installation or instrumentation; both emulator processes exited and the claim was released. That attempt did not test the three-start limit.

On `4a46dad`, strict offline verification passed 1,646 JVM tests and exact debug/test/unsigned-release packaging. The [current standard build](../../validation/providers/ocr-standard-build.md#foreground-recovery-after-temporary-ocr-refusal) then passed both retained-guest Files journeys, with and without pack-lock refusal. The exact same pair observed three native interruptions and a persisted terminal failure with no fourth start, followed by both explicit one-start reruns of that page. A separate first-Activity-launch test proved backfill for a previously saved unqueued page. Both emulator processes exited after each claim and the allocator released the guest. The release boundary remains open: no native diagnostic containment or redistribution/notice clearance, standard fresh installation, phone coverage, or representative real-document acceptance is implied.

Reuse pinned Android `Bitmap.compress(PNG)` and strict `ImageDecoder` (API28+ readback; older platforms refuse explicitly), not a custom PNG producer, permissive `BitmapFactory`, or the import filter as the export profile proof. The writer borrows the complete caller-retained raster and a synchronous byte-limited sink supplied by the file owner; it does not open/close/retain the sink or propagate source metadata. Reuse the existing opaque-sRGB row-packing pattern and one injected `ImageAllocationBudget`; charge each phase before allocation, include temporary rows/bitmaps/copies/encoded readback and workspace with checked arithmetic, recycle only private unshared bitmaps, release on failures/cancellation, and record these as provisional accounting, not a measured native footprint. Do not catch away the staged sink's explicit byte-limit or cancellation. Keep byte-count/SHA-256 of the closed PNG distinct from canonical raster identity. Read-only verification under a caller's retained stable closed-file ownership compares file-byte identity across platform decode and exact RGB row comparison; no final READY is minted.

Require a bounded streaming structural/privacy pass before Android decode: signature, exact positive expected dimensions read widened, legal direct RGB8 or fully opaque RGBA8 (opacity confirmed on actual decode), compression/filter/interlace all zero, CRC, first unique IHDR, contiguous nonempty-in-aggregate IDAT with bounded chunk count, empty terminal IEND at physical EOF. Only narrowly defined known benign fields from the preserved PNG draft may be supported; document the accepted subset and guard each extent before read. If carrying the whole proposed subset, recognize only one correctly placed each of `sRGB`, exact sRGB `gAMA`/`cHRM`, valid `pHYs`, optional suggested `PLTE`, and minimal identity-only `eXIf`; accept no text/private metadata, animation, color-profile/HDR, transparency chunks or unknown carriers. Do not confuse reported decoder header color with encoded metadata. Actual decode must use unscaled software sRGB, no partial acceptance, validate format/size/final color/opacity and compare all channels against the exact borrowed RGB with no stride padding. Preserve original oracle drafts and adapt at consumption: independently built RGB control first, standalone identity EXIF if supported, pre-IDAT text decoy, truthful corrupt-IDAT prerequisite observing the strict header callback plus reopened file audit, otherwise defer the pixel-stage claim. Count compile-only evidence as compile-only. Writer output chunks and native color, exact pixel/error stages, device resources and tagged-source nonpropagation require future authorized runtime evidence; do not widen admitted profile on speculation.
